Abstract: Organizing long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging because existing methods either suffer from expensive question-agnostic offline summaries or naive embedding similarity matching that introduces incomplete and redundant context. To address these issues, we propose GraphMemix, a combinatorial-optimization graph memory framework that models memory organization as query-aware evidence-forest construction. Specifically, our method consists of three key components:(1) candidate graph construction, which expands multi-view seed memories through schema and semantic relations to acquire query-aware original context; (2) evidence utility and activation costs, which decouples direct memory support from anchor-conditioned relation verification to suppress redundant or conflicting information; and (3) forest optimization, which jointly selects a forest-format memory context under a maximum evidence budget and its reliable relational structure. By organizing memory into a query-relevant subgraph, the method avoids substantial lifecycle cost and recovers low-similarity complementary evidence. Experimental results across four long-term multimodal memory benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements with different foundation models and establish a new Pareto frontier between accuracy and lifecycle cost.

</details>


### [165] [DSA: Evidence-Aware LLM-Agent Orchestration for Multi-Market Stock Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26990)
*Linsen Zhu,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DSA，一个面向多市场股票研究的证据感知编排框架，使用LLM agent将工作流组织为证据采集、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析、可选角色与策略技能推理以及报告生成等阶段，并包含默认报告档案与可选的agentic档案，后者通过角色解析器、信号资格分区和保守风险覆盖来保障输出安全。实现包含六个区域市场路径、十五个捆绑策略技能，并通过1,457个离线后端契约测试验证其架构符合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽可总结金融信息，但一个可操作性的股票研究系统必须首先整合异构证据、暴露不可用数据与模型能力边界，并控制生成意见如何影响最终报告。现有方法缺乏对证据获取、上下文构建与意见控制全流程的编排。

Method: DSA框架：将工作流划分为证据获取、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析、可选角色与策略技能推理、以及带选择上下文和诊断信息的报告生成。提供默认报告档案和可选agentic档案，两者共享证据与模型路由服务，但各自使用特定的输出验证和风险保障。agentic档案中，核心角色输出经角色特定解析器处理，策略技能意见需经过信号资格分区再综合，分歧被显式提供给决策agent，随后执行保守风险覆盖。参考实现包括六个区域市场路径、十五个捆绑策略技能、托管与本地模型路由，以及多个执行和交付界面。

Result: 在冻结的软件快照上，1,457个可移植离线后端契约测试全部通过；596个案例被回溯映射到六个契约族，这些契约族涉及所报告的LLM-agent架构。该证据确立了所测试软件契约的实现一致性，而非报告质量、预测准确性或投资回报的优越性。

Conclusion: DSA框架通过分层编排与契约测试验证了LLM-agent股票研究系统的工程实现可靠性，但其评估边界明确限定于实现符合性，不对报告质量或投资效果作出优越性宣称，体现了审慎的实证态度。

Abstract: Large language models can summarize financial information, but an operational stock-research system must first assemble heterogeneous evidence, expose unavailable data and model capabilities, and control how generated opinions affect a final report. We present DSA, an evidence-aware orchestration framework for multi-market stock research with large language model (LLM) agents. DSA organizes the workflow into evidence acquisition, structured context construction, model-routed analysis, optional role and Strategy Skill reasoning, and report generation with selected context and diagnostics. A default report profile and an optional agentic profile share evidence and model-routing services but use profile-specific output validation and risk safeguards. In the agentic profile, core role outputs are processed by role-specific parsers, whereas Strategy Skill opinions undergo an additional signal-eligibility partition before synthesis; disagreement is supplied explicitly to the decision agent, followed by a conservative risk override. The reference implementation includes six regional market paths, fifteen bundled Strategy Skills, hosted and local model routes, and multiple execution and delivery surfaces. At a frozen software snapshot, a selected manifest of 1,457 portable offline backend contract tests passed; 596 cases were retrospectively mapped to six contract families central to the reported LLM-agent architecture. This evidence establishes implementation conformance for the tested software contracts, not superior report quality, forecasting accuracy, or investment returns.

</details>


### [166] [ASIL: Replacing Screenshot-and-Click with Structured State and Semantic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26991)
*Rui Xie,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASIL（代理-软件交互层），一种用结构化JSON观测和可执行语义操作替代截图-点击的Agent原生软件接口，在15个应用、380个任务上达到80%+成功率和<5个动作/任务，远超截图-点击基线，并通过小规模SFT和RL进一步提升小模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体虽然能执行脚本、调用工具、管理文件，但许多重要应用仍主要通过GUI访问。截图-点击范式存在根本缺陷：截图信息不完整（state-incomplete），GUI操作脆弱、语义薄弱，且难以支撑长时程规划。因此需要一种更高效的Agent-软件交互方式。

Method: 引入ASIL接口，通过结构化JSON观察和代码可执行的语义操作暴露软件功能，对每个应用采用最深可行的访问路径（如API、UNO、MCP等底层接口）实现。在15个应用上实例化，构建了300个单应用和80个多应用任务的基准，并用closed models进行评测，同时探索大规模小模型（Qwen3.5-2B/9B）的SFT和on-policy RL训练。

Result: 在修复后的运行时和50步截图预算下，ASIL以<5个动作/任务达到80%+成功率；截图-点击控制下同一任务仅6.6/26.6（严格/宽松）和15.0/53.3（OSWorld可比子集）。与应用原生接口对比，ASIL比LibreOffice UNO API高28-38严格得分点，与draw.io MCP持平。小规模SFT将Qwen3.5-2B从58.0提至72.1，Qwen3.5-9B从66.6提至80.4；进一步RL提升至74.4和82.2。

Conclusion: 截图-点击不是软件操作智能体的最优界面，ASIL式的结构化、语义化、深度访问的Agent原生接口显著优于截图-点击基线，其结构化模态还天然适合模型训练，为未来软件操作智能体提供了一种新的接口范式。

Abstract: Powerful code agents can execute scripts, call tools, and manage files, yet many important applications remain accessible primarily through graphical user interfaces. We argue that screenshot-and-click is an inefficient interface for software-operating agents: screenshots are state-incomplete, and GUI actions are brittle, semantically weak, and poorly matched to long-horizon planning. We introduce ASIL (Agent-Software Interaction Layer), an agent-native interface that exposes software through structured JSON observations and code-executable semantic actions, realized through the deepest feasible access path for each application. We instantiate ASIL across 15 applications and a benchmark of 300 single-application and 80 multi-application tasks. ASIL reaches above 80 with closed models while executing fewer than five actions per task. Under a repaired runtime and a 50-step screenshot budget, the same tasks yield 6.6 and 26.6 strict success under screenshot-and-click control, rising to 15.0 and 53.3 on an easier OSWorld-comparable band. Against application-native interfaces on matched tasks, ASIL exceeds LibreOffice's UNO API by 28-38 strict points but only matches draw.io's MCP content contract. The structured modality also suits training: small-scale SFT raises Qwen3.5-2B from 58.0 to 72.1 and Qwen3.5-9B from 66.6 to 80.4, and resource-limited on-policy RL further raises them to 74.4 and 82.2.

</details>


### [167] [A Multi-Modal AI Framework for Real-Time Queue Prediction, Management and Optimisation in Intelligent Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27010)
*Varvara Mama,Eleni Veroni,Nikolaos Kapsalis,Christos D. Nikolopoulos,Anargyros T. Baklezos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合多模态人工智能与优化技术的边境控制管理程序，利用LSTM网络进行实时队列预测，结合模型预测控制（MPC）与调度优化产生控制策略。在合成数据上的评估表明，该方法相比ARIMA和基于规则的方法，队列预测误差降低35%，平均等待时间减少30%，吞吐量提升近20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边境控制的队列管理系统基于静态数据处理，无法适应动态交通条件和不确定性场景，导致性能欠佳。因此需要一种能够感知动态交通状况、在不确定性情况下仍保持最优性能的边境管理方案。

Method: 提出一个多模态AI框架，该框架集成异构数据源并通过统一表示呈现；采用LSTM网络进行队列预测，利用模型预测控制（MPC）与调度优化推导可行的控制策略，最终展示给边境管理官员执行。

Result: 在模拟真实交通的合成数据上进行评估，结果显示该方法将队列预测误差减少最高35%，平均等待时间降低30%，平均吞吐量相较ARIMA和基于规则的方法提升近20%。

Conclusion: 将AI架构与优化技术相结合，能够实现主动式、自适应的边境交通管理，验证了该组合方案在效率与有效性方面的显著优势。

Abstract: In the present work an efficient border control management procedure is proposed. Compared to operational queue management systems, whose operations are based on mostly static data, the proposed work takes into account dynamic traffic conditions, thus enabling optimal performance, even in cases of uncertainty. To this end, we are proposing a multi-modal Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework, tailored to th needs of border control systems, which enables real-time queue prediction, management, and resource optimization. The novel proposed approach integrates heterogeneous data sources and presents them through a unified representation by employing Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for queue forecasting. Furthermore, it leverages Model Predictive Control (MPC) and scheduling optimization to derive actionable control policies, which in turn can be presented to border control officers. The proposed work has been evaluated using synthetic data simulating realistic traffic. The evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed method reduces queue prediction error by up to 35% and average waiting time by 30%. Accordingly, the average throughput increases by nearly 20%, compared to ARIMA and rule-based methods. The abovementioned results show the effectiveness and efficiency of combining AI architectures with optimization techniques for proactive and adaptive border traffic management.

</details>


### [168] [Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27044)
*Wei-Yao Wang,Kazuya Tateishi,Shuyang Cui,Christian Simon,Takashi Shibuya,Shusuke Takahashi,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniUE（全模态交互通用嵌入器），这是首个支持文本、视频、音频统一嵌入空间且允许用户通过文本、视觉区域和音频片段进行交互式查询的模型。同时引入OmniCHOIR基准，验证OmniUE在全模态交互检索上的优势，平均提升10.5%-83.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的嵌入器主要关注语言和图像模态，缺乏对视频、音频的统一支持，且用户交互查询方式局限。作者希望突破传统两塔架构和单一模态限制，构建一个真正"全模态+全交互"的通用嵌入器。

Method: OmniUE利用可学习token的中间层表示在文本、视频、音频间建立统一嵌入空间；通过视觉和音频segmenter处理多样用户交互输入（文本、视觉RoI、音频片段），并结合omni-LLM及上下文聚合生成用户条件化的任意对任意（any-to-any）嵌入。

Result: OmniUE在多个基准上均超越SOTA：文本交互视频基准（MMEB-v2-video）平均提升10.5%，音频任务（MAEB）提升1.1%，视觉交互基准（SCaR）提升83.7%，新提出的OmniCHOIR基准提升24.1%。

Conclusion: 同时推进全模态表示学习与全模态交互查询是实现通用嵌入器的关键方向；OmniUE证明了这一路径的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has been shifting from traditional two-tower architectures to large language model (LLM)-based embedders due to their strong instruction-following capabilities. Despite this progress, existing approaches primarily focus on language and image modalities, which also remain the dominant modalities for user-conditioned interactions in current embedders. In this paper, we propose the first Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder (OmniUE), which not only learns a unified embedding space across text, video, and audio by leveraging intermediate-layer representations from dedicated learnable tokens, but also supports omni-interactive querying, enabling users to provide inputs in the form of text, visual regions of interest, and audio spans. Within OmniUE, visual and audio segmenters process diverse user interactions and integrate them with an omni-LLM to produce user-conditioned any-to-any embeddings via context aggregation. To evaluate OmniUE's omni-interactive capabilities, we introduce OmniCHOIR, benchmarking models for omni-interactive compositional audio retrieval based on the given text, video, and audio as well as unimodal or multimodal interaction prompts. OmniUE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art baselines across diverse modalities, with average improvements of 10.5% on textual-interactive video benchmarks (MMEB-v2-video), 1.1% on audio tasks (MAEB), 83.7% on visual-interactive benchmarks (SCaR), and 24.1% on our omni-interactive OmniCHOIR benchmark. We believe that jointly advancing omni-modal representation learning and omni-interactive querying paves the way toward universal embedders.

</details>


### [169] [A Contract-Centered Architecture for Scalable and Manageable Agentic Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27086)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Zhenghe Hou,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业AI部署中四个责任对象（Skill、Harness、Scaffold及数据子层）作为组织间共享契约，并给出一个有界可证伪假设P1（成本感知的能力-容量可分离性），辅以集群-周期随机交叉实验协议来判定P1是否可判定。论文目前尚无实现、实验、数据集或实测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署本质是跨业务部门、应用与AI团队、测试、平台工程、基础设施、安全、运维及数据治理的协调问题。现有的用例基准只能衡量单个agent完成单个任务的能力，无法回答能力、模型、运行时机制、容量与企业数据应如何被共同拥有、变更、准入及取证的问题。

Method: 提出四个责任对象作为共享组织契约：Skill（可复用、版本化的能力与工作流资产）、Harness（运行时编译器与治理器）、Scaffold（执行/控制边界与非功能需求所有者）、以及栈外数据子层（由CIO独立治理的语义与遥测）。运行时核心为A=<S,H,X>，数据子层位于栈外。核心贡献是一个有界可证伪假设P1（成本感知的能力-容量可分离性），六个设计条件转化为可测量义务，通过集群-周期随机交叉实验（平衡顺序、重置/清洗、重复种子与故障模式、集群感知不确定性）给出四态判决：支持、证伪、条件工程、无结论。

Result: 本文报告了契约有界的运行时架构、源保持的数据子层以及可证伪的测量协议三项贡献，但明确未报告任何已完成的实现、实验、数据集或实测结果。

Conclusion: 本文是一篇设计/理论性论文，贡献在于从组织契约视角重新定义企业AI部署的协调问题，并提出可证伪的测量框架；其价值在于为后续实证研究提供了清晰的操作化路径，但当前阶段仍缺乏实证验证。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployment is a coordination problem across business units, application and AI teams, testing, platform engineering, infrastructure, security, operations, and data governance. Use-case benchmarks show whether one agent completes one task, but not how changing capabilities, models, runtime mechanisms, capacity, and enterprise data should be owned, changed, admitted, or evidenced together.
  We present four responsibility objects as shared organizational contracts: Skill (reusable, versioned capability and workflow asset), Harness (runtime compiler and governor), Scaffold (execution/control boundary and NFR owner), and a stack-external data substrate under independent CIO-governed semantics and telemetry. The runtime core is A = <S, H, X>, with the data substrate outside that stack.
  The central contribution is one bounded, falsifiable hypothesis, P1 (cost-aware capability-capacity separability): within a declared operating region, changing activated capability preserves the capacity-response interaction within a preregistered equivalence margin, while changing compatible Scaffold capacity preserves capability semantics up to a non-inferiority margin, and the required controls stay within a declared enforcement budget. Six design conditions become measured obligations whose coverage, violations, uncertainty, cost, and exclusions determine whether P1 is decidable.
  We propose a cluster-period randomized crossover experiment (balanced order, reset/washout, repeated seeds and failure regimes, cluster-aware uncertainty) with a four-state verdict: supported, falsified, conditional-engineering, or inconclusive. This paper contributes a contract-bounded runtime architecture, a source-preserving data substrate, and a falsifiable measurement protocol. It reports no completed implementation, experiment, dataset, or measured result.

</details>


### [170] [pro-team at LLMs4OL 2026 Tasks Flagship and Reuse: Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Vocabulary-Constrained Filtering for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27101)
*Shivam Mishra,Dhannu Ram Meena,Muneendra Ojha,Krishna Pratap Singh,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离线检索增强的少样本提示流水线，用于解决LLMs4OL 2026挑战中的本体学习任务（Task A和Task B），使用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct结合all-MiniLM-L6-v2进行演示检索，并采用左截断上下文窗口策略和词汇约束过滤，在Task B上取得较好性能但在非分类关系抽取上存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在多个自然语言处理任务上取得显著进展，本体学习仍面临领域术语幻觉、格式不一致以及对层级关系（associative relations）偏好等问题，亟需提出更可靠的解决方案以提升本体学习的效率和准确性。

Method: 采用离线检索增强的少样本提示流水线：使用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct作为生成模型，all-MiniLM-L6-v2进行语义演示检索（Task A选top-5，Task B选top-2示例）；利用左截断上下文窗口策略在长提示中保留任务指令；对于Task B，对生成的三元组执行基于确定性词汇约束的过滤，保留至少一个端点属于样本封闭词/类型词汇表的三元组，并去除与初始本体重复的条目。

Result: Task B上取得语义图相似度0.8692、术语类型F1为0.9200、分类发现F1为0.8540；Task A的语义图相似度为0.7416。然而，系统未能提取任何非分类关系，暴露出封闭的、面向分类关系词汇表的局限性。

Conclusion: 检索增强的少样本提示方法在本体学习任务中表现良好，特别是在分类发现和术语类型标注方面；但当前方法受限于封闭的分类导向关系词汇，无法有效抽取非分类（关联）关系，未来需扩展关系词汇覆盖范围或引入更灵活的关系抽取机制。

Abstract: Ontology learning from text remains challenging despite significant progress in Large Language Models (LLMs), which can hallucinate domain terms, produce inconsistent formats, and favor hierarchical over associative relations. In the LLMs4OL 2026 Challenge, we address both the End-to-End Flagship Task (Task A) and Ontology Extension Reuse Task (Task B) using an offline retrieval-augmented few-shot prompting pipeline. Our system employs Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 for demonstration retrieval, selecting the top-5 examples for Task A and top-2 for Task B. A left-truncated context-windowing strategy preserves task instructions within long prompts. For Task B, generated triples undergo deterministic vocabulary-constrained filtering, retaining triples when at least one endpoint belongs to the sample's closed term/type vocabulary and removing duplicates of the initial ontology. The approach achieves Semantic Graph Similarity of 0.8692, Term-Typing F1 of 0.9200, and Taxonomy Discovery F1 of 0.8540 on Task B, while Task A achieves 0.7416 Semantic Graph Similarity. However, no non-taxonomic relations are extracted, highlighting limitations of closed, taxonomy-oriented relation vocabularies.

</details>


### [171] [LAAF: A Layered Accountability Architecture Framework for LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27102)
*Prachi Chaturvedi,Shahnawaz Ahmad,Ehsan Nowroozi,Muhammad Waqas,George Loukas,Alireza Jolfaei,Lucas Cordeiro,Pierre Dantas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述（PRISMA指南，检索2022年1月至2026年3月五个数据库，纳入122项主要研究及12份监管标准文件），构建了面向大型语言模型（LLM）的问责性（accountability）社会技术框架。综述将问责概括为"行动者-论坛"关系并分解为五个维度，综合了四类机制（技术控制、人类监督、组织治理、文档与可追溯性），并映射到欧盟AI法案、NIST AI RMF等监管框架，最终提出整合性问责架构LAAF，同时识别出四项持续性缺口与五项结构性张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已在医院、法庭、银行等关键社会场景中运行，其流畅且自信的输出即使缺乏依据或错误也被视为权威。当这种输出导致伤害时，责任归属和追溯机制缺乏系统性理解，需要整合分散的证据来回答"谁负责、通过何种机制追责、解释和执行"的问题。

Method: 遵循PRISMA指南，检索2022年1月至2026年3月期间的五个数据库，针对四个综述问题筛选出4,512条记录，最终纳入122项主要研究及12份作为主要来源分析的监管与标准文件。综述整合形成问责的社会技术解释（actor-forum关系，分解为五个维度），综合四类机制家族并给出成熟度评估；构建涵盖溯源、应用逻辑、人类监督、治理与救济的四层分类装置，并交叉可追溯性、角色清晰度和持续监控三个横切维度；随后映射到EU AI Act（2026年8月2日起高风险义务适用）、NIST AI RMF及其生成式AI画像、ISO/IEC 42001及医疗、消费金融、教育和公共部门行业指南。

Result: 识别出四项持续缺口：人类监督规范不足、缺乏共享问责指标、学科间脱节、实证评估有限；同时发现五项结构性张力是现有任何工具都无法解决的。最终将分类装置整合为统一问责架构LAAF，并将网络安全对齐到OWASP LLM Top 10（2025）。

Conclusion: LAAF是基于调查证据的综合产物而非经过验证的实用构件，表明该领域仍需实证验证与跨学科协作来推进LLM问责性研究的落地。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) operate in hospitals, courtrooms, banks, and public service desks, where fluent, confident outputs are treated as authoritative even when ungrounded or incorrect. When such an output contributes to harm, who is answerable, and through what mechanisms can responsibility be traced, explained, and acted upon? Following PRISMA guidance, five databases were searched from January 2022 to March 2026 against four review questions; of 4,512 records identified, 122 primary studies were included, together with 12 regulatory and standards documents analysed as primary sources. The review consolidates a sociotechnical account of accountability as an actor-forum relation resolved into five dimensions, and synthesises mechanisms across four families: technical controls, human oversight, organisational governance, and documentation and traceability, each with a maturity assessment. The corpus is read through a four-layer classification device spanning provenance, application logic, human oversight, and governance and redress, cross-cut by traceability, role clarity, and continuous monitoring. Both are mapped onto the EU AI Act, whose high-risk obligations have applied since 2 August 2026, the NIST AI RMF with its Generative AI Profile, ISO/IEC 42001, and sectoral guidance in healthcare, consumer finance, education, and the public sector. Four persistent gaps emerge: under-specification of human oversight, absence of shared accountability metrics, disciplinary disconnection, and limited empirical evaluation, alongside five structural tensions that no surveyed instrument resolves. The review closes by consolidating the classification device into an integrated accountability architecture, LAAF, with cybersecurity aligned to the OWASP LLM Top 10 (2025); it is a synthesis of the surveyed evidence rather than a validated artefact.

</details>


### [172] [TransMeme: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Cultural Meme Transcreation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27127)
*Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Zifan Peng,Tianyi Hu,Yuemeng Zhao,Xinhu Zheng,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了跨文化模因转创（cross-cultural meme transcreation）任务，提出了一个多智能体框架，通过文化适配、目标文本改写、修订和条件性视觉调整四个专门智能体协同工作，在双向中英模因转创任务上显著超越所有基线，人类评估中平均提升33.1%，LLM-as-Judge评估中Top-1率60%对26%。错误分析表明瓶颈在于幽默重构和图文对齐而非文化知识缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络模因是普遍的跨模态在线交流形式，但用户来自不同语言文化背景。跨文化模因转创不同于普通翻译或文本改写，需要同时保持交际意图、适配目标受众的文化含义并维持图文一致性，这一核心挑战此前缺乏系统性的任务分析和解决方案。

Method: 首先提供跨文化模因转创的显式任务分析，识别出三个核心挑战：文化特有知识理解、意图与语气保持、多模态一致性。基于此提出多智能体框架，包含文化适配、目标文本改写、修订和条件性视觉调整四个专门智能体，通过协调反馈机制增强目标文本适配能力，应对需要更深层文化或视觉干预的困难案例。

Result: 在双向中英模因转创上分别使用人工评估和LLM-as-a-Judge评估。方法在所有基线上一致胜出：人工评估中四个维度均为最优，较最强基线平均提升33.1%；LLM-as-a-Judge中Top-1排名率达60%（第二强基线仅26%）。消融分析显示每个组件均有贡献，错误分析表明主要瓶颈在于幽默重构和图文对齐。

Conclusion: 跨文化模因转创的关键挑战不仅是文化知识缺口，更在于幽默重构与图文对齐。多智能体协作框架是应对该任务的有效范式，未来工作应聚焦幽默迁移相关技术。

Abstract: Internet memes are a pervasive form of multimodal online communication; however, such communication often involves users from diverse linguistic and cultural backgrounds. Therefore, adapting memes across cultures and languages is a central challenge for enabling mutual understanding in online communication. Unlike ordinary translation or standalone text rewriting, cross-cultural meme transcreation must jointly preserve communicative intent, adapt culture-dependent meaning for the target audience, and maintain coherence between text and image. In this work, we first provide an explicit task analysis of cross-cultural meme transcreation and identify three core challenges: culture-specific knowledge understanding, intent and tone preservation, and multimodal consistency. Based on this analysis, we propose a multi-agent framework with specialized agents that are coordinated to address these challenges through cultural adaptation, target text rewriting, revision, and conditional visual adjustment. The framework strengthens target text adaptation with coordinated feedback to handle difficult cases that require deeper cultural or visual intervention. We evaluate the framework on bidirectional Chinese-English meme transcreation using both human evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge. Our method consistently outperforms all baselines across both evaluation settings. In human evaluation, it achieves the best performance on all four dimensions and delivers a 33.1% average improvement over the strongest baseline, while in LLM-as-a-Judge, it attains the highest Top-1 ranking rate (60% versus 26% for the second-best baseline). Further analysis indicates that each component contributes to the performance. Our error analysis suggests that the remaining bottlenecks lie in humor reconstruction and image-text alignment rather than simple cultural knowledge gaps, pointing to future work on humor transfer.

</details>


### [173] [GRAIN: Bridging Name and Narrative Shifts in Real-World Graph Reasoning through Invariance-Rewarded Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27142)
*Zike Yuan,Han Zhang,Jianzhi Yan,Le Liu,Cai Ke,Huozhi Zhou,Jian Xie,Jiran Yin,Yukun Cao,Yue Yu,Hui Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAIN，一个基于强化学习的单智能体框架，通过结构不变性奖励（Structure Invariance Reward）引导LLM进行语义解析和工具执行，以解决LLM在图任务中对节点标识符和任务表述变化敏感的问题。同时引入GRIT基准测试评估语言偏移的敏感性，GRAIN在准确率上比多智能体基线提升16.45%，延迟降低约24%，并将分布外（OOD）差距从15.77%减半至7.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在标准化图任务中表现良好，但面对真实的节点标识符变化和任务表述变化时十分脆弱。确定性图工具对这些变化具有不变性，但从噪声文本中提取拓扑结构对LLM而言高度困难，且容易过拟合表面模式。此外，通过多智能体系统缓解解析失败会带来难以接受的延迟开销，因此需要一个既能保持鲁棒性又能控制延迟的解决方案。

Method: GRAIN是一个通过强化学习优化的单智能体框架。它将推理建模为语义解析和工具执行的流水线，并由结构不变性奖励（Structure Invariance Reward）引导。该奖励通过将提取的中间图与ground-truth拓扑进行验证，迫使LLM学习鲁棒的文本到结构映射而非记忆语言表面特征。同时构建GRIT基准来评估模型对语言偏移的敏感性。

Result: GRAIN在多智能体基线上准确率提升16.45%，延迟降低约24%。在结构泛化方面表现优越，将SFT模型的OOD差距从15.77%减半至7.80%，并且在超出训练分布的大规模图上保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GRAIN通过强化学习驱动的单智能体设计和结构不变性奖励机制，成功解决了LLM在图任务中对语言偏移的脆弱性问题，同时兼顾了准确率与推理效率，为现实世界的图推理任务提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite their potential in standardized graph tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain brittle to real-world shifts in node identifiers and task formulation. While deterministic graph tools are invariant to such shifts, extracting topological structures from noisy text is highly fragile for LLMs, which often overfit to surface patterns. Moreover, mitigating these parsing failures via multi-agent systems incurs prohibitive latency. To address this, we propose GRAIN, a single-agent framework optimized via reinforcement learning. GRAIN models reasoning as a semantic parsing and tool-execution pipeline, guided by a Structure Invariance Reward. By validating extracted intermediate graphs against ground-truth topologies, this reward forces the LLM to learn robust text-to-structure mappings rather than memorizing linguistic artifacts. We also introduce GRIT, a benchmark evaluating sensitivity to such linguistic shifts. GRAIN outperforms multi-agent baselines by 16.45\% in accuracy with approximately 24\% lower latency. Furthermore, it demonstrates superior structural generalization, halving the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap of SFT models (from 15.77\% to 7.80\%) and maintaining robustness on large-scale graphs beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [174] [Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27144)
*Xiang Wang,Zhijun Cheng,Zhenyu Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JPGFN，一种基于自适应Jacobi多项式图滤波的图异常检测方法，通过特征分离变换网络、自适应Jacobi多项式图滤波模块和节点标签约束模块，解决了现有频率域图异常检测方法在图滤波器适应性、节点特征细粒度信息和标签利用方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频域滤波的图异常检测方法存在三个主要挑战：1) 使用静态基函数构造图滤波器，无法有效适应图数据的频域分布；2) 未充分考虑节点特征向量中各属性的重要性信息，导致细粒度信息丢失；3) 对节点标签信息的利用不足。

Method: 提出JPGFN方法，包含三个核心模块：1) 特征分离变换网络(FSTNN)，通过特征分离并对节点特征不同维度进行非线性变换来学习细粒度节点特征；2) 基于Jacobi多项式的自适应图滤波模块，自适应捕捉图信号的复杂频域特征；3) 节点标签约束模块，利用节点标签提升图异常检测性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，JPGFN方法显著优于主流方法。

Conclusion: JPGFN通过自适应多项式图滤波、细粒度特征学习和标签约束三方面的协同设计，有效解决了现有频域图异常检测方法的固有缺陷，展现了优越的检测性能。

Abstract: In recent years, graph anomaly detection (GAD) based on frequency-domain filtering have achieved promising results. However, existing approaches still face three major challenges: First, they use static basic function to constructed graph filter which cannot effectively adapt to the frequency-domain distribution of graph data. Second, they fail to adequately consider the importance information of each attribute in the node feature vector, leading to the loss of fine-grained information. Third, they insufficiently utilize node labels for GAD. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel graph anomaly detection method called JPGFN (Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering Network). First, a Feature Separation Transformation Network (FSTNN) is developed to better learn fine-grained node features by feature separation and applying nonlinear transformations to node features across different dimensions. Second, an adaptive Jacobi polynomial graph filtering module is constructed based on Jacobi polynomials to adaptively capture complex frequency-domain features of graph signals. Finally, a node label constraint module is developed to facilitate the use of node labels and enhance the performance of GAD. Experimental results on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms mainstream approaches.

</details>


### [175] [When Tool Outputs Become Commands: Separating Action Induction from Runtime Authorization in Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27146)
*Xiaokun Guo,Zhen Xu,Dongdong Huo,Yanqiu Zhang,Wei Wang,Qinfu Yang,Dongjin Yu,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SARA框架，将工具增强LLM智能体中的动作归纳（action induction）与执行授权（execution authorization）分离，以防止不可信的运行时观察输出被当作"命令"驱动超出用户意图的真实世界副作用。SARA通过上下文隔离的动作探针暴露动作归纳语义、记录来源溯源，并通过无历史提升机制防止历史复发将动作来源"洗白"为执行权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强LLM智能体必须依赖不可信的运行时观察输出来完成任务，但当工具输出不再仅仅是提供数据而开始指定具体动作时，它们实际上变成了可以驱动超出用户意图的真实世界副作用的"命令"。这一风险源于将动作归纳与执行授权混为一谈，需要用运行时角色分离来区分二者。

Method: SARA将动作归纳和执行授权作为不同的运行时角色处理，将动作来源与执行权限分离。在观察侧，上下文隔离的动作探针（Action Probe）暴露动作归纳语义并跨步骤持久记录动作来源溯源作为审查信号；在执行侧，实际工具调用仅针对用户目标以及来自授权成功执行的可审计证据进行授权，同时满足目标支持、执行链支持和参数级别支持。为在多步执行中保持此分离，SARA应用无历史提升（No-History-Promotion）防止历史复发将动作来源洗白为执行权限。

Result: 在AgentDojo和AgentDyn上，SARA在四个主要评估设置中将攻击成功率（ASR）限制在不超过0.63%，同时保持具有竞争力的任务效用，并在额外的Agent骨干网络上持续降低ASR。

Conclusion: SARA通过分离动作归纳与执行授权，有效缓解了工具增强LLM智能体中不可信观察输出带来的安全风险，在显著降低攻击成功率的同时保持了良好的任务效用，验证了动作来源与执行权限分离这一设计原则的有效性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents must rely on untrusted runtime Observations to complete open-ended tasks; however, when tool outputs no longer merely provide data but begin to specify concrete actions, they effectively become ``commands'' that can drive real-world side effects beyond user intent. We argue that this risk arises from conflating action induction with execution authorization. To address this distinction, we propose SARA, which treats action induction and execution authorization as distinct runtime roles and separates action provenance from execution authority. On the Observation side, a context-isolated Action Probe exposes action-inducing semantics and persistently records action-origin provenance across steps as a review signal; on the execution side, actual tool calls are authorized only against the user objective and audited evidence from authorized successful executions, while satisfying goal, execution-chain, and argument-level support. To preserve this separation across multi-step execution, SARA applies No-History-Promotion to prevent historical recurrence from laundering action origins into execution authority. Across AgentDojo and AgentDyn, SARA limits ASR to no more than \(0.63\%\) across four primary evaluation settings while maintaining competitive task utility, and consistently reduces ASR across additional Agent backbones.

</details>


### [176] [Thomson: Continual Learning of Frontier Models for SovereignAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27147)
*Shengzhuang Chen,Jerrod Parker,Yejin Bang,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat,Stefan Winzeck,Daniil Glazko,Jannik Zgraggen,Fangyi Yu,Scott Arnott,Dietrich Trautmann,Luca Ciuffreda,Guglielmo Bonifazi,Davide Romano,Bradley Bell,Kirsty Fielding,Daniele Giofrè,Tom Zielund,Ipshita Chatterjee,Sneha Murthy Ghantasala,Manpreet Nanreh,John Scoville,Maciej Sakowicz,Wassim Seifeddine,Lukas Thede,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过持续学习（Continual Learning）在开源权重模型基础上实现前沿性能，使更多机构能够独立构建、部署和管理自己的AI系统（主权AI）。作者展示了Thomson模型，一个专注于高风险专业工作的通用前沿模型，在智能体任务、安全、法律、税务、多语言和深度研究等多个维度上达到与近期前沿模型相当的水平，并以较少的算力和人员预算实现了跨多代模型的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型的开发通常被认为只有少数资金雄厚的机构才能完成，导致开发者与多样化用户群之间存在信息、经济和权力的不对称。尽管公共话语呼吁主权AI（机构独立构建、部署和管理AI的能力），但缺乏在短期内在多种资金条件下实现这一目标的具体建议。

Method: 采用持续学习方法，在现成的开源权重模型基础上进行改进。与传统的小规模微调、提示工程或冻结模型的工具增强不同，该方法利用现代中训练和后训练技术栈，同时引入保护机制来在每个阶段保持模型的可塑性和稳定性，对参数进行最少数量但高影响力的干预。

Result: Thomson模型在智能体任务、安全、法律、税务、多语言和大型深度研究等任务上与近期前沿模型表现相当。评估显示出独特的π形模式：在广泛能力（包括未明确目标的能力）上均有明显提升，同时几乎消除了窄领域适配中常见的遗忘问题。性能提升可与跨越多个连续模型世代的改进相当，而计算和人员预算远低于通常预期。

Conclusion: 持续学习方法使得更广泛的参与者能够拥有主权AI栈的大部分（模型、工具基础设施、价值观和数据隐私），打破了前沿模型开发被少数资金雄厚的玩家垄断的局面，为更多机构在短期内实现主权AI提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: The development of frontier models is commonly perceived to be the exclusive remit of a small number of heavily funded players, creating an information, economic and power asymmetry between developers and the diverse user base of modern AI. Recent public discourse acknowledges this concern, calling for SovereignAI (an organisation's capability to independently build, deploy and govern AI use), but offers little concrete advice on how this can be achieved in the short term under a diversity of funding settings. We argue that frontier performance is achievable by a wide range of institutions through Continual Learning on readily available open-weight models. Unlike limited approaches such as small-scale fine-tuning, prompt engineering, or tool-augmentation of a frozen model, our approach exploits a modern mid- & post-training stack while introducing safeguards that preserve both plasticity and stability at each stage, making the minimal number of high-impact interventions on the parameters. This yields gains comparable to those typically seen across multiple successive model generations, at compute and personnel budgets substantially lower than commonly thought, making ownership of large parts of the SovereignAI stack (model, tool infrastructure, values & data privacy) viable for far more actors. We demonstrate this with Thomson, a general-purpose frontier model trained with an enhanced focus on high-stakes professional work. Thomson performs competitively with recent frontier models across agentic tasks, safety, legal, tax & multilingualism, and large-scale Deep Research. Evaluations show a distinctive $π$-shaped pattern: distinct improvements across a wide range of capabilities, including those not explicitly targeted, while almost completely eliminating the forgetting problem common to narrow domain adaptation.

</details>


### [177] [BPMN4CAI: A BPMN Extension for Modeling Dynamic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27149)
*Björn-Lennart Eger,Daniel Rose,Barbara Dinter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个符合标准的BPMN扩展（BPMN4CAI），用于在业务流程中表示动态、上下文敏感的对话式AI交互，并通过案例研究验证其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BPMN标准在表示对话式AI系统（如聊天机器人和虚拟助手）的动态、上下文敏感交互方面存在挑战，而对话式AI在数字业务流程中日益重要，因此存在方法论和实践上的研究空白。

Method: 采用设计科学研究（Design Science Research）方法论，系统性地扩展现有BPMN元素并整合专用组件，开发出标准兼容的BPMN4CAI扩展框架。

Result: 通过案例研究验证了BPMN4CAI框架的适用性和相关性，结果表明该扩展能够促进对话式AI在业务流程中的自适应决策过程、稳健的上下文管理以及透明的交互。

Conclusion: BPMN4CAI扩展有效弥补了BPMN标准在表示对话式AI交互方面的不足，为对话式AI在业务流程中的建模提供了标准化的解决方案。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems, such as chatbots and virtual assistants, are becoming increasingly important to digital business processes. However, the established Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) standard faces challenges when representing dynamic, context-sensitive interactions. This paper addresses this methodological and practical research gap by developing a standard-compliant BPMN extension (BPMN4CAI). Using Design Science Research methodology, this paper develops an approach that systematically extends existing BPMN elements and incorporates specialized components. The applicability and relevance of the BPMN4CAI framework are demonstrated and evaluated through a case study. The results show that the BPMN4CAI extension facilitates adaptive decision-making processes, robust context management, and transparent interactions for Conversational AI within business processes.

</details>


### [178] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现AI智能体面对包装精美的“市场面板”时，对不可预测问题做出方向性承诺的比例从6.5%飙升至54.0%，即使面板所有数字都伪造也是如此。问题不在模型能力或信念，而在“行动/不行动”的门控机制失效。通过监督微调小型模型可将其驱动至0.0%承诺率，但该门控在僵化响应格式下会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI系统为何会对外观专业但本质上不可预测的问题做出自信承诺，定位失败的具体机制层（非能力、非信念、非判断缺失，而是行动门控），并验证该门控是否可训练及在何种条件下失效。

Method: 对12个前沿模型做对照实验：(1)裸提问vs附加专业市场面板；(2)真实数据vs完全伪造数据；(3)可回答与不可回答配对问题；(4)测量模型对可知性的主观判断与实际承诺率差异。随后用540个合成样本（骰子、硬币、罐子、计时器）对3B模型做监督微调，并跨三个未见领域检验泛化与格式鲁棒性。

Result: 承诺率随证据升级从6.5%升至54.0%；完全伪造面板仍将承诺率从24.5%提升至36.8%，与真实数据产生的37.6%无统计差异。模型对可答问题几乎全能作答且准确率接近完美；声称概率几乎不随操作承诺的梯度变化；模型90%能识别问题的不可知性却在仅0.4%的此类问题上拒答。微调后承诺率降至0.0%并跨领域迁移，但在移除推理空间的僵化格式下门控失效。

Conclusion: 驱动自信行动的不是信息本身，而是信息的“包装权威性”。失败是窄而可定位的：行动门控（act/don't-act gate）失效，且集中于少数模型。该门控可训练但具有上下文脆弱性——部署时必须同时考虑其可训练性和对格式的敏感性。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [179] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对LLM智能体交互数据生成的两层框架：首先将智能体数据表示为因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，涵盖环境、任务、交互和验证器；其次通过Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity（ACE）视角将生成问题形式化为约束分布设计。该框架统一了现有工作的生成机制，揭示了从单纯数据规模转向执行加权精度、学习者相对复杂度和超越表面多样性的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体数据生成工作分散于多个领域，组织方式以领域为中心且评估标准异质，导致常见的生成机制被模糊，候选构建与验证、选择步骤被混淆。需要一个统一的框架来理清生成范式、组织现有方法并指明未来方向。

Method: 提出了两层框架：（1）将智能体数据表示为因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，即环境规范、任务信号、交互实现和可选验证器，并按主锚点和依赖结构组织生成范式；（2）通过ACE（Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity）视角将生成形式化为约束分布设计：准确性奠定有根据且内部一致数据的可行支持集，复杂度根据学习者能力和执行配置分配学习质量，多样性控制数据覆盖率与冗余度。

Result: 通过该框架审视现有工作，识别出三大趋势：向执行锚定的精度（execution-grounded accuracy）转变、向学习者相对复杂度（learner-relative complexity）转变、以及向超越表面变化或数据集规模的多样性（diversity beyond surface variation）转变。该框架还能用于讨论缩放、数据源、训练机制和自适应学习等更广泛的方向。

Conclusion: 智能体数据生成的核心挑战不在于生成更多数据，而在于随着智能体和环境的演化，持续分配有效、信息丰富且非冗余的体验。ACE框架为这一持续性问题提供了统一的组织和分析视角。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [180] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO)，一种轻量级单谱系提示优化方法，通过教师模型与rollout反馈迭代修订提示。NPO在更少的rollout下达到或超越GEPA的性能，且优势随教师模型增强而扩大；在交互式游戏中与GEPA基本持平。NPO优化的提示可直接迁移到同族其他学生模型。结果表明简单的线性提示优化可以媲美更复杂的搜索过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agentic AI中，递归自我改进（RSI）需要高效提升自主agent在多样化任务上的能力。提示优化是比微调权重更具成本效益的途径，但近期研究趋势倾向于采用不必要复杂的提示优化器。作者旨在验证简单的提示优化方法是否足以匹敌复杂方法，以推动更高效的RSI。

Method: NPO是一种轻量级单谱系（single-lineage）迭代方法：使用教师模型生成提示修订，通过rollout反馈评估效果并逐步优化提示。作者将NPO与GEPA（更复杂的搜索式优化器）在多种任务上对比，并测试了不同教师模型强度以及将优化结果迁移到其他学生模型的效果。

Result: NPO在更少的rollout下达到或优于GEPA的表现；更强的教师模型会放大NPO的优势（强教师推理可部分替代优化器搜索复杂度）；在交互式游戏中NPO与GEPA基本持平，GRPO在部分不太适合提示优化的任务上表现更好；NPO优化的提示在应用到同族其他学生模型时仍能带来类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，简单线性的提示优化可以媲美（甚至在某些情况下超越）显著更复杂的搜索式优化方法，这为递归自我改进提供了一条更高效、更具可扩展性的路径。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [181] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BrailleBench基准，用于评估大型语言模型（LLMs）在布莱叶盲文理解方面的能力。该基准包含5,570个样本，涵盖数学、常识和多跳问答五个数据集，跨越英语以及盲文一级和二级标准。通过确定性、专家审核的流程构建，评估了六个代表性LLM，揭示了印刷英语能力与盲文可访问性之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型应平等地为弱势群体（特别是盲人和聋盲用户）提供访问知识和计算辅助的能力，但现有AI系统在盲文理解方面是否足够包容尚不清楚。盲文的指示符、缩略语和数字表示对模型理解提出了独特要求，而缺乏针对性的评估基准。

Method: 作者创建了BrailleBench基准，从五个数据集对齐5,570个实例，设计不同配置以测试模型对盲文内容的理解、以盲文表达答案以及端到端盲文交互能力。基准通过自建的Braille Toolkit以确定性、专家审核的流程构建，完全避免使用LLM生成的数据实例。评估了六个代表性LLM在多个方面的表现。

Result: 实验结果揭示了印刷英语能力与盲文可访问性之间存在持续的差距。盲文理解与表达表现出不对称性：二级盲文在输入端尤为脆弱（相比一级盲文），而完全盲文请求进一步降低模型性能。

Conclusion: 实验观察为未来盲文AI系统的发展提供了有价值的指导。BrailleBench的所有相关资源已公开，可供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [182] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否为规范化的运筹学（OR）问题设计有效算法，涵盖库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化三类问题。该研究表明，最强的模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上匹配或超越了现有最佳方法，即便在第二层级（仅给定问题类别描述和参数范围，需返回固定算法）也能达到这一水平，且该能力在短短八个月内快速提升，表明前沿LLM可作为规范化OR问题算法设计的严肃经验基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLMs是否能在无需大量人类专家干预的情况下，为结构化明确的运筹学问题设计出具有竞争力的算法，从而评估LLM作为算法设计工具的潜力。

Method: 在三个规范化OR问题（库存控制、排队网络控制、品类优化）上，在两个层级评估LLM：层级1为给定单个实例返回解决方案；层级2为仅给定问题类别描述和宽泛参数范围，返回一个从实例参数映射到解决方案的固定算法。人类输入仅为单个未调优的问题描述提示词，模型可使用Python沙盒工具并受固定计算预算限制。

Result: 最强模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评估实例上匹配或超越了现有最佳方法，且在层级2（算法在见到评估实例前就已固定）也成立。此外，相隔不到八个月发布的模型在性能上出现显著提升，表明该能力发展迅速。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的规范化OR问题，一次未经调优的LLM查询已经能够生成与专门方法竞争的算法，前沿LLM可以成为规范性OR问题算法设计的一个严肃的经验基线。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [183] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HarnessLens，一个预算感知的自动化 Agent 框架（harness）演化框架，通过联合探索任务空间与可配置组件、从执行轨迹推导候选修改、并利用可归因证据门控对行为相关任务进行选择性验证，从而在受限交互预算下更高效、更可靠地优化语言模型 Agent 框架。在三个 harness 和四个基准上平均提升 7.6-13.6% 的留出集性能，同时显著节省评估预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 propose-and-verify 方法在固定任务集上对所有候选进行评分，浪费大量 rollout 在与无关行为上，且聚合指标会掩盖特定回归问题；同时适配 Agent harness 需要昂贵的验证成本。因此需要一种预算感知、行为级验证的框架，在受限预算下可靠地演化 harness。

Method: HarnessLens 包含三个核心设计：1) 联合探索任务空间与用户可配置组件（joint exploration）；2) 从执行轨迹（execution trajectories）中推导候选修改；3) 利用可归因证据门控（attributable-evidence gate）仅在与行为相关的任务上选择性验证每个候选，从而避免无关行为上的浪费性评估。

Result: 在三个 Agent harness 和四个基准上，HarnessLens 将平均留出集性能提升 7.6%-13.6%，同时相比竞争基线消耗显著更少的评估预算。

Conclusion: 带有显式归因（attribution）的行为感知验证能够实现更可靠且更具样本效率的 harness 演化，尤其适用于受限交互预算的场景。这表明验证策略从"全量评分"转向"按行为归因的选择性验证"是提升框架演化效率的关键。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [184] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现思维链中口头表达的"评估意识"（eval-awareness）并非单一量，而是可分为能力框架、安全框架、两者兼具或两者皆非等多种类型，这些框架对模型顺从行为的预测能力差异显著。在Qwen3-32B和FORTRESS数据集上，能力框架相比安全框架对顺从行为的预测差距达+24到+46个百分点，且通过CoT-prefill干预证实了因果联系。评估意识在行为上并不均匀，总体抑制率的变化可能与安全相关成分的变动脱钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估管线中，针对评估意识的steering干预日益普遍，但现有方法将评估意识视为单一可抑制量，忽略了其内部可能存在不同语义框架，导致安全评估结果可能失真。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B模型上使用FORTRESS数据集，对思维链中口头化的评估意识进行框架分类（能力、安全、两者、皆非）；通过CoT-prefill干预对评估意识阴性样本进行因果验证。

Result: 能力框架预测顺从行为的差距比安全框架高+24到+46个百分点（所有测试steering条件下成立）；10/11的prefill干预使顺从行为按预测方向偏移；评估意识的总体抑制率变化可能掩盖安全相关成分不变的情况。

Conclusion: 评估意识不是行为上单一均匀的量，当前以总体抑制率衡量steering效果的方法可能产生误导性结论，安全评估需对评估意识进行细粒度框架区分。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [185] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于一家大型企业8个月期间约4000名后台员工、713,564条提示词及大语言模型响应的专有数据，考察生成式AI使用的成熟度差异。发现：资深员工使用更成熟；成熟度在职能部门间差异显著（战略、数字创新和项目管理最高）；使用成熟度随时间无改善，正式AI培训也未带来持久提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在企业后台劳动力中的应用日益广泛，但对其使用成熟度（sophistication）的个体与组织层面差异缺乏系统性理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，为管理者改进AI应用效果及研究者开展后续研究提供量化依据。

Method: 利用某大型企业的专有数据，涵盖2025年8个月间近4000名跨15个职能领域的后台员工所提交的713,564条提示词及其对应的LLM响应，对生成式AI使用的成熟度进行量化测度与分析。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)资深员工表现出的genAI使用成熟度更高，支持领域专长与genAI能力互补的假设；(2)成熟度在职能部门间差异显著，战略、数字创新和项目管理三个部门最高，这三者均聚焦于公司级战略举措与组织变革；(3)使用成熟度随时间无改善，正式AI培训也未带来持久提升，表明成熟使用较难改变。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对成熟genAI使用的量化测度与洞察，可供管理者优化AI使用效果，也为未来研究提供了基础。同时暗示仅靠培训可能难以提升成熟度，需探索其他干预机制。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [186] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CorporateBench (CB)，一个大规模、人工验证的多任务Q&A基准测试，评估语料库超过23万份文档，模拟从小型到大型企业（12至10,000名员工）的通信网络。实验发现LLM在输入规模接近现实尺度时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在企业级文档问答能力日益增强，但评估困难：企业不愿共享内部通信数据，现有合成数据集过于简单，无法反映真实企业通信网络的复杂度与规模。因此需要一个大规模、逻辑一致且人工验证的基准。

Method: 构建四个合成企业（员工数从12到10,000不等），每个语料库从随时间演化的知识库中采样，确保跨文档逻辑一致性。基准涵盖两个维度：信息抽取和知识库查询。数据经过人工验证，并评估了五个LLM。

Result: 评估五个LLM后发现，当输入规模接近现实尺度时，模型性能显著下降，表明现有LLM在企业规模文档推理方面仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: CB填补了企业通信推理基准的空白，为LLM开发者提供评估企业级文档推理能力的有效指标，揭示了LLM在真实规模企业场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


### [187] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于患者轨迹学习的脓毒症严重程度指数，利用43个常规记录变量和72小时治疗窗口，以死亡率为排序信号而非逐状态目标进行训练。在29,116和7,691名Sepsis-3成年患者（来自马萨诸塞州和佐治亚州两个医院系统）的回顾性双队列研究中，该指数在0-10分制上较幸存者高1.19-1.64分（在各SOFA-2基线分层内），与乳酸变化相关（Spearman rho=0.39），跨机构一致性为同站点相关性的70-77%，外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59（上限0.92和0.90）。作者认为该指数提供每小时预后信息且与临床预期一致，有望作为临床判断的辅助决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脓毒症严重程度指标（如SOFA）依赖数十年前校准的固定变量和权重，离散化粗糙，且其校准人群已不能反映现代重症监护实践。目前没有直接从患者轨迹中学习的替代指标被常规使用，因此需要一种从真实临床数据中学习、能反映当代重症监护特征的脓毒症指数。

Method: 采用回顾性双队列研究设计，纳入29,116例（马萨诸塞州）和7,691例（佐治亚州）符合Sepsis-3标准的成人患者。使用43个常规记录的变量，覆盖72小时治疗窗口。关键创新在于使用死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号而非逐状态目标，允许贡献非均匀地在时间步间重新分配。评估使用固定的20%测试集保留，采用临床病例分析和Spearman相关分析，通过整患者自助重采样获得不确定性区间。

Result: 在所有SOFA-2基线分层内，非幸存者评分比幸存者高1.19-1.64分（0-10分制），在乳酸、MAP和肌酐分层中结果相似。患者内指标变化与乳酸变化相关（Spearman rho=0.39，n=1,854），与MAP和肌酐的相关性较弱。跨机构一致性为同站点相关性的70-77%，外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59（上限分别为0.92和0.90）。该指数与既有指标相关，而零对照接近零。

Conclusion: 该脓毒症指数提供逐小时的预后信息，能够有意义地区分患者结局，且与临床预期一致，表明其作为辅助临床判断的决策支持工具具有潜力。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [188] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAELLE是一种将化学反应建模为电子占据向量上的离散流匹配（Discrete Flow Matching）的新方法，通过在CTMC框架下对键合、非键合和氢位点的整数电子占据空间进行编辑轨迹构造，无需基元步骤标注即可实现机械化可解释的反应预测，在USPTO-480K基准上取得竞争性表现，并在分布外场景和机制轨迹恢复方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习反应预测方法要么通过de novo生成产物分子，要么基于分子拓扑进行启发式图编辑，二者都未直接建模化学反应的本质——电子空间的变换。作者希望在电子层面建模反应，从而获得更根本的化学理解、更好的泛化鲁棒性以及可解释的机制轨迹。

Method: MAELLE将反应物到产物的映射形式化为定义在所有成键、非键合和氢位点上的图结构整数电子占据空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。通过将离散流匹配混合路径推广到离散电子重排场景，并利用最优传输（Optimal Transport）构造中间编辑轨迹，从而生成一系列机械化可解释的编辑动作，无需基元步骤标注。

Result: 在USPTO-480K基准上取得与领先反应预测模型相当的竞争性性能。在结构复杂性和反应类型两种分布外设置中，MAELLE保持强健性能而现有方法性能下降。此外，模型能够自然恢复与已知化学一致的机制轨迹，并可预测反应的副产物。

Conclusion: MAELLE证明了在电子占据空间上直接建模化学反应的优势：不仅实现了竞争性的反应预测精度，还带来了更强的分布外鲁棒性、机械化可解释的轨迹恢复能力以及副产物预测能力，为反应预测提供了超越传统拓扑编辑范式的化学本质视角。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [189] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WikiSkill框架，将代理技能与持久化知识库（wiki）共同演化，系统性地从原始执行经验中提炼知识，再基于知识更新技能，从而提升技能演化的效率和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动技能发现方法虽然能让代理通过交互逐步适应，但驱动技能开发的洞察通常散落在优化历史中，难以在多次迭代中被系统地重用，限制了技能演化的效率和效果。

Method: WikiSkill将原始执行经验、累积知识和可执行技能三层分离，持续将经验整合进wiki知识库，后续的技能更新可以在已有知识基础上进行构建，实现技能与知识库的共同演化。

Result: 在多种基准和模型上，WikiSkill一致优于最先进的技能演化方法；在大多数模型-基准组合中优于无技能基线。技能演化与模型规模互补，更大的模型从演化技能中获益更多，而带技能的小模型可超越不带技能的更大模型。演化技能可跨模型和模型家族迁移，其他模型演化的技能有时优于自我演化技能。消融实验证实wiki中的持久知识积累对有效技能演化至关重要。

Conclusion: 系统性地累积和精炼代理经验对于开发可重用、可迁移的技能具有重要价值，持久化知识库是技能高效演化的关键组件。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [WALL-SS: Scaling Long-horizon World Models via Next-Scale Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26239)
*Maeve Zhang,Rain Sun,Xiang Wang,Cyril Zhang,Shalfun Li,Meng Cao,Howard Lu,Ethan Chen,Harry Jhou,KZ Zheng,Lights Shi,Regis Cheng,Lorenzin,Robert Wang,Victor Yao,Gody Li,Elise Mon,Yohann Tang,Ryan Yu,PS Zhang,Vincent Chen,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WALL-SS是一个基于尺度层次自回归的世界模型，通过将具身轨迹表示为因果序列，实现动作可控、长时程的机器人视觉仿真，并支持变长生成和流式扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型能为机器人提供预测性的环境演化模型，但现有方法局限于片段级未来预测，缺乏统一的生成式框架来关联动作与后果、支持灵活时域和连续交互、以及实现奖励驱动的优化。

Method: WALL-SS采用尺度层次自回归（Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling）生成视觉未来。具体包括三个互补组件：(1) 动作条件下一尺度预测（Action-conditioned next-scale prediction）注入尺度对齐的动作表示，增强动作-未来耦合；(2) 尺度压缩长时程记忆（Scale-compressed long-horizon memory）在细分辨率保留近期交互、压缩远距离观测与动作，并采用尺度级梦想强迫提升对自生成上下文的鲁棒性；(3) 在策略对齐（On-policy alignment）通过动作跟随与长期一致性奖励优化自回归视觉动力学，同时保持预训练视觉分布。

Result: 实验表明WALL-SS提升了动作跟随能力和轨迹精度，支持在有界内存下进行连贯的分钟级流式生成，且在策略对齐能一致地减少动作漂移和长时程不一致性问题。

Conclusion: WALL-SS通过统一的尺度层次自回归生成框架，将动作与后果显式关联，为机器人的长时间仿真、规划与策略评估提供了可行且高效的世界模型方案。

Abstract: Generative world models provide robots with predictive models of how the world evolves under interaction, with growing potential for simulation, planning, policy evaluation, and robot learning. Beyond clip-level future prediction, a unified generative formulation should relate actions to consequences, support flexible horizons and continuous interaction, and enable reward-driven optimization. We introduce WALL-SS, a world model that generates visual futures through Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling, enabling action-controllable and long-horizon robotic simulation. WALL-SS represents embodied trajectories as causal sequences of temporally interleaved observations and actions, making action-dependent state transitions explicit while naturally supporting variable-length generation, streaming extension through reusable causal states, and direct optimization through sequence probabilities. To make this formulation effective over long horizons, we generate each future observation in a coarse-to-fine manner and develop three complementary components within the same hierarchy. Action-conditioned next-scale prediction injects scale-aligned action representations to improve action-future coupling and model both successful and failed behaviors. Scale-compressed long-horizon memory retains recent interactions at fine resolution while compressing distant observations and actions, with scale-wise dream forcing enhancing robustness to self-generated context. Finally, on-policy alignment optimizes autoregressive visual dynamics with action-following and long-term consistency rewards while preserving the pretrained visual distribution. Experiments show that WALL-SS improves action following and trajectory accuracy, supports coherent minute-long streaming rollout under bounded memory, and consistently benefits from on-policy alignment in reducing action drift and long-horizon inconsistency.

</details>


### [191] [Constraint-Aware Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Static Shape Estimation of Co-Manipulative Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26273)
*Rana Danesh,Pari Qarehdaghi,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束感知物理信息神经网络（PINN）的方法，用于闭环协同操作连续体机器人（CCR）的静态形状估计。该方法通过投影静态平衡残差和几何闭环残差来强制满足力学和几何约束，相比纯数据驱动的ANN在有限且含噪数据下显著降低误差，且推理速度远快于迭代非线性求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同操作连续体机器人（CCR）的静态形状估计具有挑战性，因为连续体臂与被操纵的柔性物体形成闭环，必须同时满足静态平衡和几何闭环约束。传统方法依赖迭代非线性求解器，计算开销大，而纯数据驱动方法在有限含噪数据下表现不佳。

Method: 提出一种约束感知的物理信息神经网络（PINN），基于几何可变应变公式对腱驱动CCR建模。网络训练中融入两类物理约束：(1) 投影静态平衡残差，用于强制力学平衡；(2) 配置级几何残差，用于强制闭环几何一致性。同时支持使用实验结果对网络进行微调。

Result: 在140个样本且50%标签噪声条件下，相比纯数据驱动ANN，相对配置误差、平衡残差和闭环残差分别降低67.88%、67.35%和88.06%。使用完整数据集时，相对配置误差达0.1597%，推理时间0.1773ms，而迭代非线性求解器需17.97s。实验微调后将标记点RMSE从2.657mm降至0.497mm，R2从-0.788提升至0.937。

Conclusion: 该PINN方法实现了闭环CCR静态形状估计的准确性、物理一致性和计算高效性，在有限且含噪数据下显著优于纯数据驱动方法，并能通过实验微调进一步提升实际部署性能。

Abstract: Static shape estimation of co-manipulative continuum robots (CCRs) is challenging because the continuum arms and manipulated flexible object form a closed chain that must satisfy both static equilibrium and geometric loop-closure constraints. This paper presents a constraint-aware physics-informed neural network (PINN) for static shape estimation of a tendon-driven CCR modeled using the geometric variable strain formulation. The proposed method incorporates a projected static equilibrium residual and a configuration-level geometric residual to enforce the governing mechanics and closed-chain geometry. In simulation, the PINN is compared with a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN) under limited and noisy training data. With 140 samples and 50% label noise, the PINN reduces the relative configuration error, equilibrium residual, and closed-chain residual by 67.88%, 67.35%, and 88.06%, respectively. Using the full dataset, the PINN achieves 0.1597% relative configuration error with an inference time of 0.1773 ms, compared with 17.97 s for an iterative nonlinear solver. Experimental fine-tuning reduces the marker RMSE from 2.657 mm to 0.497 mm and increases R2 from -0.788 to 0.937. These results demonstrate accurate, physically consistent, and computationally efficient static shape estimation of closed-chain CCRs.

</details>


### [192] [Dispersive Forward Tree Search for Optimal Control: Coverage, Complexity, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26314)
*Shashank A. Deshpande,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前向传播的运动规划器 DFT*（Dispersive Forward Tree search），针对微分平坦非线性系统提供确定性的有限样本近最优性保证。通过构造局部色散控制命令集，前向树在证书规模内包含近最优轨迹，并采用成本条件优势剪枝实现多项式规模树，可在并行硬件上高效实现且在嵌入式处理器上优于现有最优运动规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于转向（steering）的规划器需要求解状态到状态的边界值问题（BVP），对于非线性平台往往不可行；而前向传播方法虽然规避了这一要求，但其有限样本行为缺乏理论刻画，实际实现性能也不佳。

Method: 面向微分平坦非线性系统，构建前向树和局部色散控制命令集；为控制仿射系统提供色散命令集的通用构造机制；提出成本条件优势剪枝过程，将树规模从指数级降低至多项式级；实现为广度优先搜索的前向树扩展（DFT*），天然适配并行硬件。

Result: 理论上证明了覆盖证书轨迹类在无成本条件下需要指数规模的树，而成本条件优势剪枝可保持近最优性并在多项式规模下实现。为独轮车、拖挂车和四旋翼设计了高效色散采样器，DFT* 在嵌入式处理器上以相当求解时间取得一致竞争且经常显著更优的解决方案质量，并行缩放下加速更明显；在滚动时域循环中实现了动态环境实时规划。

Conclusion: DFT* 是第一类具备确定性有限样本近最优性保证的传播式运动规划算法，兼具理论保证与实际高效实现，并验证了在嵌入式计算预算下实时动态环境规划的能力。

Abstract: Steering-based planners require solutions to state-to-state boundary value problems, which can be inaccessible for nonlinear platforms. Forward propagation evades the steering requirement, but the finite-sample behavior of the associated planners remains uncharacterized and their implementations underperform in practice. This paper develops a propagation-based kinodynamic planner with deterministic finite-sample near-optimality guarantees. We work within the large class of differentially flat nonlinear systems and show that a forward tree of locally dispersive control commands contains a near-optimal trajectory at a certified tree size. We provide a general mechanism to construct dispersive command sets for control-affine systems, which are necessary to implement the search algorithm prescribed by the theory. We show that covering the certified trajectory class irrespective of cost provably demands a tree exponentially sized in the problem horizon, and present a cost-conditioned dominance pruning procedure that retains near-optimality at a tree size polynomial in the horizon. We implement the resulting search algorithm, Dispersive Forward Tree search (DFT*), as breadth-first expansion of the forward tree, which maps naturally onto parallel hardware. We design efficient dispersive samplers for the unicycle, the trailer car, and the quadrotor and evaluate challenging planning tasks for these platforms. DFT* delivers consistently competitive and often substantially better solution quality than state-of-the-art kinodynamic planners at comparable solution times on embedded-tier processors, accelerating further as parallel compute is scaled. We also implement DFT* in a receding-horizon loop to demonstrate real-time planning in dynamic environments at embedded-tier compute budgets.

</details>


### [193] [Cross-Platform Benchmark of Neural 3D Reconstruction for Autonomous Laboratory Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26383)
*Yongho Kim,Mengjiao Han,Victor Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Michael E. Papka,Nicola Ferrier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地对神经3D重建方法（NeRF和3D高斯泼溅）在多种GPU计算平台上进行了基准测试，涵盖从单板计算机到服务器级节点的范围，并与Meta的SAM3D单图重建方法进行了对比。结果表明高斯泼溅在渲染质量上优于NeRF但GPU开销更大，板载计算无法满足交互速率下的完整逐场景优化，而SAM3D能在数秒内给出合理几何但细节存在偏差。研究建议采用分层管线：轻量级前馈重建维持实时感知与追踪，重型神经重建在合适算力上选择性调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室机器人执行任务依赖能在物理控制回路延迟预算内完成三维重建的管线，但神经重建方法在实验室机器人实际运行的计算平台上的实时可行性缺乏系统性评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，为机器人系统的计算资源规划提供依据。

Method: 作者构建了一个系统性的计算平台基准测试框架，在GPU计算设备谱系（从单板计算机到服务器级节点）上评估NeRF和3D高斯泼溅的训练与渲染性能，同时将Meta的SAM3D单图重建放在相同评估维度上，量化其与逐场景优化方法之间的延迟和保真度差距。

Result: 高斯泼溅在渲染质量上优于NeRF，但GPU计算成本更高；在板载计算条件下，完整逐场景优化无法达到交互速率；SAM3D能在数秒内产生合理的物体几何，但在细节上与真值存在偏差，可能影响下游操作任务。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持分层重建管线的设计思路：以轻量级前馈重建支撑实验室机器人的实时感知与追踪环路，而将计算密集型的神经重建安排在合适的计算资源上选择性执行。

Abstract: Autonomous robots performing laboratory tasks depend on 3D reconstruction pipelines that can turn raw camera streams into actionable object representations within the latency budget of a physical control loop. Neural 3D reconstruction methods have demonstrated high-quality view synthesis, but their real-time viability across the compute platforms on which laboratory robots actually run remains poorly characterized. In this work, we present a systematic compute-platform benchmark of neural 3D reconstruction methods, evaluating NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting training and rendering on GPU-enabled computing devices ranging from single-board computers to server-class nodes, and place Meta's SAM3D single-image reconstruction on the same axes to quantify its latency and fidelity gap relative to per-scene optimization. Our results show that Gaussian Splatting yields higher rendering quality than NeRF at greater GPU cost, and that onboard compute is insufficient for full per-scene optimization at interactive rates. Our preliminary assessment on SAM3D indicates that it delivers plausible object geometry within seconds, but with detail mismatches that can compromise downstream manipulation. Together, these findings motivate tiered pipelines in which lightweight feed-forward reconstruction sustains the real-time perception-and-tracking loop for laboratory robots, while heavier neural reconstruction is scheduled selectively on suitable compute.

</details>


### [194] [RTNav: Towards Real-Time Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26496)
*Easop Lee,Lingyu Zhang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RTNav架构，将推断延迟、异步环境步进和有限计算资源作为显式设计考量，解决了真实世界实时导航中视觉-语言基础模型推断延迟导致的性能退化问题，在实时HM3D基准上成功率最高提升11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法大多在同步仿真器中开发，环境会等待智能体行动，推断时间被视作零成本。然而在真实世界实时执行中，墙钟时间计入任务预算，视觉-语言模型的推断延迟会导致系统性的性能退化，这一关键问题此前未被充分重视。

Method: 提出RTNav架构，将推断延迟（inference latency）、异步环境步进（asynchronous environment stepping）和有界计算（bounded compute）作为显式设计考量，使架构设计围绕时间约束而非单纯追求感知-推理-行动的串行效率展开。

Result: 在实时变体的HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON基准上，RTNav相比先前方法将成功率最高提升11%，任务完成时间加权成功率（SCT）最高提升5.1个点。

Conclusion: 同步仿真器会掩盖真实世界实时导航中的推断延迟问题，将时间约束纳入架构设计能显著改善零样本目标导航在真实执行条件下的性能。

Abstract: Navigation in unknown environments to find unforeseen objects has become increasingly feasible with capable vision and language foundation models. However, these models also introduce non-negligible inference latency, which becomes an important concern when agents must operate continuously in the real world. Most state-of-the-art methods are still developed in synchronous simulators, where the environment waits for the agent to act and inference time is effectively free. As a result, agents are often designed around the sequential execution of perception, reasoning, and action, with little regard for time constraints. Under real-time execution, where wall-clock time counts towards the task budget, the inefficiencies of these architectures become clear. We show that recent zero-shot object navigation methods suffer consistent performance degradation under such realistic timing conditions. Motivated by this observation, we propose RTNav, a simple but effective architecture that treats inference latency, asynchronous environment stepping, and bounded compute as explicit design considerations. Evaluated on real-time variants of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, RTNav improves the success rate by up to 11% and the Success weighted by Completion Time by up to 5.1 points over prior work.

</details>


### [195] [Closing the Loop on the Poppy Humanoid: Bipedal Locomotion with Linear-Quadratic Control and Learned Cost Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26505)
*Xulin Chen,Borui He,Ruipeng Liu,Naveed Tahir,Zhenyu Gan,Garrett E. Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文为开源低成本机器人 Poppy Humanoid 提出了基于LQR框架的闭环行走控制器，通过从开环轨迹播放数据中学习二次代价函数，显著提升行走可靠性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poppy Humanoid 是适用于AI研究和教育的开源低成本机器人，但此前没有公开方法能在标准硬件上实现可靠、无需辅助的双足行走，这限制了其在行走研究中的应用。

Method: 基于线性二次调节器（LQR）框架进行轨迹跟踪；从标称行走轨迹的开环播放中收集数据，利用这些数据学习一个二次代价函数来构建LQR控制器。

Result: 通过实验验证了闭环控制器的有效性，与开环轨迹播放相比，行走性能在统计上具有显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功为Poppy标准硬件实现了可靠的功能性闭环行走控制器，可作为该平台双足行走研究的基础。

Abstract: The Poppy Humanoid is an open-source, low-cost robot suitable for research and education in artificial intelligence. However, we are unaware of any published methodology that achieves reliable, unassisted bipedal locomotion on the standard Poppy hardware. This paper contributes a functional closed-loop walking controller for Poppy, based on the linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) framework for trajectory tracking. Starting with data collected from open-loop playback of a nominal walking trajectory, our proposed method learns a quadratic cost function for an LQR controller that substantially improves the reliability of the motion. The closed-loop controller is validated empirically, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in walking performance compared to open-loop trajectory playback.

</details>


### [196] [Memory Anchors for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26545)
*Maximilian Du,Zhanyi Sun,Chen Xu,Paarth Shah,Masha Itkina,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现经验回放缓冲区中只有少数样本（称为Memory Anchors）对防止灾难性遗忘至关重要。这些锚点位于新旧任务表示发生塌缩但动作冲突的区域。移除仅10%的锚点会使LIBERO基准上的遗忘增加4.5倍以上，而丰富缓冲区中的锚点可将高冲突任务遗忘降低63%，并支持真实机器人上的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放环境中部署的机器人策略需要持续学习新任务而不遗忘已有行为，但随机采样的回放缓冲区效率低下，因为并非所有旧经验对防止灾难性遗忘同等重要。作者希望识别并利用关键经验子集以更高效地缓解灾难性遗忘。

Method: 作者识别出一类特殊的经验样本——Memory Anchors，它们位于新旧任务观测表示发生表征塌缩（collapse）但任务要求冲突动作的区域。基于此，作者在构造回放缓冲区时优先选择这些锚点样本，而非随机均匀采样。通过消融实验（排除锚点）和富集实验（增加锚点）验证其在防止遗忘中的作用。

Result: 在LIBERO基准套件上，从回放缓冲区中排除仅10%的Memory Anchors就导致灾难性遗忘增加超过4.5倍；相反，在回放缓冲区中富集Memory Anchors可将高冲突任务的遗忘降低63%，并成功实现真实机器人上两个任务序列的持续学习。

Conclusion: 回放缓冲区中的经验并非同等重要，Memory Anchors是防止旧任务知识被破坏性覆盖的关键锚点。在缓冲区构建中优先保留这些锚点样本可以显著提升持续学习性能，为面向真实场景的机器人持续学习提供了一种高效策略。

Abstract: Robot policies deployed in the wild should have the capability to continually learn new tasks without forgetting existing behaviors. A common approach to combat such catastrophic forgetting is to train on new task data with a replay buffer of previously learned task data. Although this buffer is commonly sampled randomly from all prior experiences, we show that a small set of these experiences contributes greatly in anchoring past performance. We call these experiences Memory Anchors. We identify Memory Anchors in regions where representations of new-task observations collapse onto those of old-task observations even though the tasks require conflicting actions, like when a familiar object must be manipulated in a new way. Rehearsing old data in this region plays a key role in preventing destructive overwriting of past task knowledge, serving as this critical Memory Anchor role. Excluding only 10% Memory Anchors before sampling the buffer leads to more than a 4.5x increase in catastrophic forgetting on the LIBERO benchmark suites. Conversely, enriching the replay buffer with Memory Anchors can decrease high-conflict task forgetting by 63% and enables successful continual learning of two task sequences on a real robot. Videos and additional visualizations can be found at https://robot-adaptation.github.io/MemoryAnchors

</details>


### [197] [TrapVLA: Trapping Vision-Language-Action Models in Configured Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26578)
*Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Yi-Lin Wei,Xu-Han Chen,Yinghao Li,Zhuohao Li,Yuan-Ming Li,Qing Zhang,Xiaoyi Fan,Dongmei Jiang,Yan Li,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO
